Appendix A
Appendix: Preliminary Results and Example Verification

This appendix provides useful lemmas.
It also verifies the examples in Subsection
2.4
.

A.1
Useful lemmas

We recall some useful results which will be used in our convergence analysis.

Lemma 11
(
Bauschke2011
, Lemma 5.31)

Let
\{u_{k}\}
,
\{v_{k}\}
,
\{\gamma_{k}\}
, and
\{\varepsilon_{k}\}
be nonnegative sequences such that
A_{\infty}:=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\gamma_{k}<+\infty
and
B_{\infty}:=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\varepsilon_{k}<+\infty
.
In addition, for all
k\geq 0
, we assume that

u_{k+1}\leq(1+\gamma_{k})u_{k}-v_{k}+\varepsilon_{k}.

(86)

Then, we conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}u_{k}
exists and
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}v_{k}<+\infty
.

Lemma 12
(
TranDinh2025a
)

Given a nonnegative sequence
\{u_{k}\}
and
\omega\geq 0
such that
\lim_{k\to\infty}k^{\omega+1}u^{k}
exists and
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}k^{\omega}u_{k}<+\infty
.
Then, we conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}k^{\omega+1}u^{k}=0
.

We also need the well-known Robbins-Siegmund supermartingale theorem
robbins1971convergence
, which we state it here as a technical lemma.

Lemma 13

Let
\{U_{k}\}
,
\{\alpha_{k}\}
,
\{V_{k}\}
and
\{R_{k}\}
be sequences of nonnegative integrable random variables on some arbitrary probability space and adapted to the filtration
\left\{\mathcal{F}_{k}\right\}
with
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\alpha_{k}<+\infty
and
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}R_{k}<+\infty
almost surely, and

\mathbb{E}\big[U_{k+1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{k}\big]\leq(1+\alpha_{k})U_{k}-V_{k}+R_{k},

(87)

almost surely for all
k\geq 0
.
Then, almost surely,
\{U_{k}\}
converges to a nonnegative random variable
U
and
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}V_{k}<+\infty
almost surely.

The following lemma is proven similarly to
(
combettes2015stochastic
, Theorem 3.2.)
and
(
davis2022variance
, Proposition 4.1.)
.
We recall it here without repeating the proof to cover the composite mapping
\Phi
in (
GE
).

Lemma 14

Suppose that
\mathrm{gra}(\Phi)
of
\Phi
in (
GE
) is closed.
Let
\{x^{k}\}
be a sequence of random vectors such that for all
x^{\star}\in\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)
, the sequence
\{\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\}
almost surely converges to a
[0,\infty)
-valued random variable.
In addition, assume that
\{\|w^{k}\|\}
for
(x^{k},w^{k})\in\mathrm{gra}(\Phi)
also almost surely converges to zero.
Then,
\{x^{k}\}
almost surely converges to a
\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)
-valued random variable
x^{\star}\in\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)
.

A.2
Verifying examples in Subsection
2.4

Example 2
.
We provide more detailed explanation of
Example 2
in Subsection
2.4
.
First, using Jensen’s inequality, it is easy to check that
G
satisfies (
3
) in Assumption
3
with
\alpha=1
.
Second, for all
x,y\in\mathrm{dom}(\Phi)
, let
w_{x}:=Gx+v_{x}
and
w_{y}:=Gy+v_{y}
, where
v_{x}\in Tx
and
v_{y}\in Ty
, respectively.
Then, from Young’s inequality, we have
(1+\tau)\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}-\|v_{x}-v_{y}\|^{2}\geq-\frac{(1+\tau)}{\tau}\|w_{x}-w_{y}\|^{2}
for any
\tau>0
.
Utilizing the assumptions in
tran2024accelerated
;
TranDinh2025a
, Jensen’s inequality, and the last relation, we can easily prove that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\langle w_{x}-w_{y},x-y\rangle&=&\langle Gx-Gy,x-y\rangle+\langle v_{x}-v_{y},x-y\rangle\\
&\geq&\frac{1}{L}\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{G}_{\xi}x-\mathbf{G}_{\xi}y\|^{2}\big]-\rho\|v_{x}-v_{y}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&\big[\frac{1}{L}-(1+\tau)\rho\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{G}_{\xi}x-\mathbf{G}_{\xi}y\|^{2}\big]+\rho\big[(1+\tau)\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}-\|v_{x}-v_{y}\|^{2}\big]\\
&\geq&\big[\frac{1}{L}-(1+\tau)\rho\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{G}_{\xi}x-\mathbf{G}_{\xi}y\|^{2}\big]-\frac{(1+\tau)\rho}{\tau}\|w_{x}-w_{y}\|^{2}.\end{array}

This inequality shows that
\Phi:=G+T
studied in
tran2024accelerated
;
TranDinh2025a
satisfies (
6
) in Assumption
4
with
\rho_{n}:=\frac{(1+\tau)\rho}{\tau}\geq 0
,
0\leq\rho_{c}\leq\frac{1}{L}-(1+\tau)\rho
.
Clearly, to guarantee
\rho_{c}\geq 0
, we require
0\leq L\rho\leq\frac{1}{1+\tau}<1
.

Example 4
.
Since
R(x,y)\leq M
, we have
n\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|G_{i}x-G_{i}y\|^{2}\leq M\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(G_{i}x-G_{i}y)\|^{2}
.
This implies that
\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{G}_{\xi}x-\mathbf{G}_{\xi}y\|^{2}\big]=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|G_{i}x-G_{i}y\|^{2}\leq\frac{M}{n^{2}}\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(G_{i}x-G_{i}y)\|^{2}=M\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}
.

Now, since
G
is
\rho
-co-hypomonotone, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\langle Gx-Gy,x-y\rangle&\geq&-\rho\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}=-\rho_{n}\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}+(\rho_{n}-\rho)\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}\\
&\geq&-\rho_{n}\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}+\frac{\rho_{n}-\rho}{M}\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{G}_{\xi}x-\mathbf{G}_{\xi}y\|^{2}\big].\end{array}

Note that in order for the last inequality to hold (and to match the condition (
7
) in Assumption
4
), we require
\rho_{n}>\rho
.
In this case, we get
\rho_{c}:=\frac{\rho_{n}-\rho}{M}
.

Example 5
.
By the definitions of
\bar{\sigma}_{i}
and
\underline{\sigma}
, we have
\|G_{i}x-G_{i}y\|\leq\bar{\sigma}_{i}\|x-y\|
and
\|Gx-Gy\|\geq\underline{\sigma}\|x-y\|
, respectively.
Combining these two relations, we get
\|G_{i}x-G_{i}y\|\leq\frac{\bar{\sigma}_{i}}{\underline{\sigma}}\|Gx-Gy\|
.
This implies
\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{G}_{\xi}x-\mathbf{G}_{\xi}y\|^{2}\big]:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|G_{i}x-G_{i}y\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bar{\sigma}_{i}^{2}\right)\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}=\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}
, where we redefine
\bar{\sigma}^{2}:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bar{\sigma}_{i}^{2}
.

Now, since
G
is
\rho
-co-hypomonotone, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\langle Gx-Gy,x-y\rangle&\geq&-\rho\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}=-\rho_{n}\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}+(\rho_{n}-\rho)\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}\\
&\geq&-\rho_{n}\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}+\frac{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}(\rho_{n}-\rho)\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{G}_{\xi}x-\mathbf{G}_{\xi}y\|^{2}\big].\end{array}

Note that in order for the last inequality to hold (and to match the condition (
7
) in Assumption
4
), we require
\rho_{n}>\rho
.
In this case, we also get
\rho_{c}:=\frac{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}(\rho_{n}-\rho)
.

Appendix B
Appendix: The Proof of Lemmas
4
,
5
, and
6

This appendix presents the full proof of the three lemmas: Lemmas
4
,
5
, and
6
.

B.1
The proof of Lemma
4

First, using
e^{k}
and
\hat{e}^{k}
from (
44
), we can write
\widetilde{G}y^{k}=Gy^{k}+e^{k}
and
\widetilde{G}y^{k-1}=Gx^{k}+\hat{e}^{k}
.
Denote
w^{k}:=Gx^{k}+v^{k}
and
\hat{w}^{k}:=Gy^{k-1}+v^{k}
for a given
v^{k}\in Tx^{k}
.
Then, we can rewrite the first and third lines of (
VFOG
) equivalently to

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}x^{k+1}&=&sz^{k}+(t_{k}-s)x^{k}-\eta t_{k}(\hat{w}^{k+1}+e^{k})+\beta_{k}t_{k}(w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k}).\end{array}

Rearranging this expression in two different ways, and then using
sz^{k}=sz^{k+1}+\gamma_{k}(\widetilde{G}y^{k-1}+v^{k})=sz^{k+1}+\gamma_{k}(w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k})
from the last line of (
VFOG
), we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)(x^{k+1}-x^{k})&=&s(t_{k}-s)(z^{k}-x^{k})-\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)(\hat{w}^{k+1}+e^{k})+\beta_{k}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)(w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k}),\\
t_{k}(t_{k}-s)(x^{k+1}-x^{k})&=&st_{k}(z^{k}-x^{k+1})-\eta t_{k}^{2}(\hat{w}^{k+1}+e^{k})+\beta_{k}t_{k}^{2}(w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k}),\\
&=&st_{k}(z^{k+1}-x^{k+1})-\eta t_{k}^{2}(\hat{w}^{k+1}+e^{k})+t_{k}(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma_{k})(w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k}).\end{array}

Second, by the condition (
6
) of
\Phi
in Assumption
4
and using the notion
|\!\|\cdot|\!\|
, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle-t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle&\geq&-\rho_{n}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}.\end{array}

Combining the last three expressions, we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[1]}&:=&s(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle-st_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-z^{k+1}\rangle\\
&\geq&\eta t_{k}^{2}\langle w^{k+1},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle-t_{k}(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma_{k})\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle-\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\beta_{k}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\rho_{n}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}+\eta t_{k}\langle t_{k}w^{k+1}-(t_{k}-s)w^{k},e^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\beta_{k}t_{k}\langle t_{k}w^{k+1}-(t_{k}-s)w^{k},\hat{e}^{k}\rangle-\gamma_{k}t_{k}\langle w^{k+1},\hat{e}^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Now, by Young’s inequality, for any
c_{1}>0
and
c_{2}>0
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[2]}&:=&\langle t_{k}w^{k+1}-(t_{k}-s)w^{k},e^{k}\rangle\\
&\geq&-\frac{c_{1}}{2t_{k}}\|t_{k}w^{k+1}-(t_{k}-s)w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{t_{k}}{2c_{1}}\|e^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&-\frac{c_{1}t_{k}}{2}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{c_{1}(t_{k}-s)^{2}}{2t_{k}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+c_{1}(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle-\frac{t_{k}}{2c_{1}}\|e^{k}\|^{2},\\
\mathcal{T}_{[3]}&:=&-\langle t_{k}w^{k+1}-(t_{k}-s)w^{k},\hat{e}^{k}\rangle\\
&\geq&-\frac{c_{2}\eta}{2\beta_{k}t_{k}}\|t_{k}w^{k+1}-(t_{k}-s)w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\beta_{k}t_{k}}{2c_{2}\eta}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&-\frac{c_{2}\eta t_{k}}{2\beta_{k}}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{c_{2}\eta(t_{k}-s)^{2}}{2\beta_{k}t_{k}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{c_{2}\eta(t_{k}-s)}{\beta_{k}}\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle-\frac{\beta_{k}t_{k}}{2c_{2}\eta}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2},\\
\mathcal{T}_{[4]}&:=&-\langle w^{k+1},\hat{e}^{k}\rangle\geq-\frac{1}{2}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Substituting
\mathcal{T}_{[2]}
,
\mathcal{T}_{[3]}
, and
\mathcal{T}_{[4]}
into
\mathcal{T}_{[1]}
, we can further derive that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[1]}&:=&s(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle-st_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-z^{k+1}\rangle\\
&\geq&\eta t_{k}^{2}\langle w^{k+1},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle-\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle+\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&{~}-\frac{t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big[2(\rho_{n}-\beta_{k})+\frac{c_{1}\eta(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}+\frac{c_{2}\eta(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&{~}-\frac{t_{k}}{2}\big[2\rho_{n}(t_{k}-s)+c_{1}\eta t_{k}+c_{2}\eta t_{k}+\gamma_{k}\big]\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}t_{k}\big[2\rho_{n}(t_{k}-s)+c_{1}\eta(t_{k}-s)+c_{2}\eta(t_{k}-s)-\beta_{k}t_{k}-\gamma_{k}\big]\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2c_{1}}\|e^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{t_{k}}{2}\big(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}t_{k}}{c_{2}\eta}+\gamma_{k}\big)\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Next, assume that
(c_{1}\eta+c_{2}\eta+2\rho_{n})(t_{k}-s)-t_{k}\beta_{k}-\gamma_{k}=0
.
This condition is guaranteed if we update
\beta_{k}
as in (
45
), i.e.:

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\beta_{k}=\frac{(c_{1}\eta+c_{2}\eta+2\rho_{n})(t_{k}-s)-\gamma_{k}}{t_{k}}.\end{array}

Partly substituting this
\beta_{k}
into
\mathcal{T}_{[1]}
and using the following identity

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle&=&\frac{t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\end{array}

we can simplify the last expression
\mathcal{T}_{[1]}
as

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[1]}&:=&s(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle-st_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-z^{k+1}\rangle\\
&\geq&\eta t_{k}^{2}\langle w^{k+1},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle-\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&{~}-\frac{t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big[2\rho_{n}+\eta+\frac{(c_{1}+c_{2})\eta(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}-\frac{2(c_{1}\eta+c_{2}\eta+2\rho_{n})(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}+\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{t_{k}}\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&{~}-\frac{t_{k}}{2}\big[(c_{1}+c_{2})\eta t_{k}+\gamma_{k}+2\rho_{n}(t_{k}-s)\big]\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2c_{1}}\|e^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{t_{k}}{2}\big(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}t_{k}}{c_{2}\eta}+\gamma_{k}\big)\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}+{~}\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}.\end{array}

For a given
\omega\geq 0
, we define
M^{2}:=2(1+\omega)L^{2}
.
Then, from the second and third lines of (
VFOG
), we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}(x^{k+1}-y^{k})=-\eta t_{k}(\hat{w}^{k+1}+e^{k})+\eta t_{k}(w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k})=-\eta t_{k}(\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k})-\eta t_{k}(e^{k}-\hat{e}^{k}).\end{array}

Applying the condition (
3
) from Assumption
2
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+E_{k}=\|Gx^{k+1}-Gy^{k}\|^{2}+E_{k}\leq(1+\omega)L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2},\end{array}

where

\begin{array}[]{lcl}E_{k}&:=&(\alpha-1)\|Gx^{k+1}-Gy^{k}\|^{2}+(1-\alpha)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gy^{k}|\!\|^{2}+\omega L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&(1-\alpha)\big[|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}-\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\omega L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Combining the last two expressions, we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+t_{k}^{2}E_{k}&\leq&(1+\omega)L^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&(1+\omega)L^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}+e^{k}-\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+2M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}+2M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Multiplying both sides of this inequality by
\frac{\eta}{2}
and partly expanding the resulting expression and rearranging the result, we can deduce that

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}0&\geq&\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-\eta t_{k}^{2}\langle w^{k+1},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle+\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}E_{k}\\
&&-{~}\frac{M^{2}\eta^{3}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}-M^{2}\eta^{3}t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}-M^{2}\eta^{3}t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

Adding this inequality to
\mathcal{T}_{[1]}
yields

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[1]}&:=&s(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle-st_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-z^{k+1}\rangle\\
&\geq&\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}E_{k}+\frac{\eta t_{k}[t_{k}-s-M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}]}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big[2\rho_{n}+\eta+\frac{(c_{1}+c_{2})\eta(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}-\frac{2(c_{1}\eta+c_{2}\eta+2\rho_{n})(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}+\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{t_{k}}\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{t_{k}}{2}\Big(\eta t_{k}-\big[(c_{1}+c_{2})\eta t_{k}+\gamma_{k}+2\rho_{n}(t_{k}-s)\big]\Big)\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\big(\frac{\eta}{2c_{1}}+M^{2}\eta^{3}\big)t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}-\big(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}t_{k}}{2c_{2}\eta}+\frac{\gamma_{k}}{2}+M^{2}\eta^{3}t_{k}\big)t_{k}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

Next, utilizing
s(z^{k+1}-z^{k})=-\gamma_{k}(w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k})
from the last line of (
VFOG
) and Young’s inequality, for any
c_{k}>0
, we can derive that

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{[4]}&:=&\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k}}\|z^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&=&-\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{\gamma_{k}}\langle z^{k+1}-z^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k}}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&s(s-1)\langle w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{(s-1)\gamma_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&s(s-1)\langle w^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{s^{2}(s-1)c_{k}}{2}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(s-1)\gamma_{k}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{s-1}{2}\big(2\gamma_{k}+\frac{1}{c_{k}}\big)\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

Since
\gamma_{k}:=\frac{\gamma(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}
and
t_{k}=t_{k-1}+1
from (
45
) for some
\gamma\geq 0
, we can easily prove that

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{k-1}}-\frac{1}{2\gamma_{k}}&=&\frac{1}{2\gamma}\big(\frac{t_{k-1}}{t_{k-1}-1}-\frac{t_{k}}{t_{k}-1}\big)=\frac{(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-1)-t_{k}(t_{k}-2)}{2\gamma(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)}=\frac{1}{2\gamma(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

Therefore, if we choose
c_{k}:=\frac{1}{\gamma_{k-1}}-\frac{1}{\gamma_{k}}=\frac{1}{\gamma(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)}
, then we have
\frac{1}{2\gamma_{k}}+\frac{c_{k}}{2}=\frac{1}{2\gamma_{k-1}}
.
Using this relation, the expression
\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{[4]}
implies that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[4]}&:=&\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k-1}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k}}\|z^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s(s-1)\langle w^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{\gamma(s-1)(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}\big[\frac{2(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}+(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)\big]\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.}\end{array}

Since
t_{k}-t_{k-1}=1
due to (
45
), we have
s(t_{k-1}-t_{k}+s)=s(s-1)
.
Adding
\mathcal{T}_{[4]}
to
\mathcal{T}_{[1]}
and using
s(t_{k-1}-t_{k}+s)=s(s-1)
, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[5]}&:=&st_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle+\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k-1}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}st_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-z^{k+1}\rangle-\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k}}\|z^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}E_{k}+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s-M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k})}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{t_{k}}{2}\big[(1-c_{1}-c_{2})\eta t_{k}-\gamma_{k}-2\rho_{n}(t_{k}-s)\big]\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\Big\{\frac{t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big[2\rho_{n}+\eta-\frac{[(c_{1}+c_{2})\eta+4\rho_{n}](t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}+\frac{2\gamma(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}^{2}}\big]+\frac{\gamma(s-1)(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}\Big\}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\Big[\big(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}t_{k}}{2c_{2}\eta}+\frac{\gamma(t_{k}-1)}{2t_{k}}+M^{2}\eta^{3}t_{k}\big)t_{k}+\gamma(s-1)\big(\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t_{k}-1}}{t_{k}}+\frac{(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)}{2}\big)\Big]\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\big(\frac{\eta}{2c_{1}}+M^{2}\eta^{3}\big)t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Now, we recall from (
48
) that

{\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}a_{k+1}&:=&(1-c_{1}-c_{2})\eta t_{k}^{2}-2\rho_{n}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)-\gamma(t_{k}-1),\\
\hat{a}_{k}&:=&\big[(1-c_{1}-c_{2})\eta-2\rho_{n}\big](t_{k}-s)^{2}+s(\eta+2\rho_{n})(t_{k}-s)+\frac{2\gamma(t_{k}-1)^{2}}{t_{k}},\\
b_{k}&:=&\big(\frac{1}{2c_{1}}+M^{2}\eta^{2}\big)\eta t_{k}^{2},\\
\hat{b}_{k}&:=&\big(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}t_{k}}{2c_{2}\eta}+\frac{\gamma(t_{k}-1)}{2t_{k}}+M^{2}\eta^{3}t_{k}\big)t_{k}+\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}\big[\frac{2(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}+(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)\big].\end{array}\right.}\vskip-2.15277pt

Then, we can rewrite the last expression
\mathcal{T}_{[5]}
as

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[6]}&:=&\frac{a_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+st_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle+\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k-1}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{a_{k+1}}{2}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-st_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-z^{k+1}\rangle-\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k}}\|z^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}E_{k}+\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s-M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k})}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\frac{a_{k}-\hat{a}_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-b_{k}\|e^{k}\|^{2}-\hat{b}_{k}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

Substituting
\mathcal{P}_{k}
from (
46
) into this new expression
\mathcal{T}_{[6]}
, we eventually get (
47
).

\square

B.2
The proof of Lemma
5

First, since
s\geq 2
due to (
49
) and
t_{k}=t_{k-1}+1
due to (
45
), we have
t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\leq(t_{k}-1)^{2}
.
Thus, for
\psi:=(1-c_{1}-c_{2})\eta-2\rho_{n}
, from (
48
), we can show that

{\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}a_{k}&:=&\psi t_{k-1}^{2}+2s\rho_{n}t_{k-1}-\gamma(t_{k-1}-1)=\psi(t_{k}-1)^{2}+(2s\rho_{n}-\gamma)(t_{k}-1)+\gamma,\\
\hat{a}_{k}&:=&\psi(t_{k}-s)^{2}+s(\eta+2\rho_{n})(t_{k}-s)+\frac{2\gamma(t_{k}-1)^{2}}{t_{k}}\\
&\leq&\psi(t_{k}-s)^{2}+[s(\eta+2\rho_{n})+2\gamma](t_{k}-1)-s(s-1)(\eta+2\rho_{n}).\end{array}\right.}\vskip-2.15277pt

Using these relations, we can lower bound

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}a_{k}-\hat{a}_{k}&\geq&\psi(s-1)(2t_{k}-s-1)-(s\eta+3\gamma)(t_{k}-1)+\gamma+s(s-1)(\eta+2\rho_{n})\\
&=&[2\psi(s-1)-s\eta-3\gamma](t_{k}-1)-\psi(s-1)^{2}+\gamma+s(s-1)(\eta+2\rho_{n})\\
&=&\big[[s-2-2(c_{1}+c_{2})(s-1)]\eta-4\rho_{n}(s-1)-3\gamma\big](t_{k}-1)+\gamma+s(s-1)(\eta+2\rho_{n})\\
&&-{~}(s-1)^{2}[(1-c_{1}-c_{2})\eta-2\rho_{n}].\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

Next, let us choose
c_{1}=c_{2}:=\frac{s-2}{8(s-1)}\geq 0
.
Then, we obtain from the above expressions that

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}a_{k}&=&\big[\frac{(3s-2)\eta}{4(s-1)}-2\rho_{n}\big]t_{k-1}^{2}+2s\rho_{n}t_{k-1}-\gamma(t_{k-1}-1),\\
a_{k}-\hat{a}_{k}&\geq&\big[\frac{(s-2)}{2}\eta-4(s-1)\rho_{n}-3\gamma\big](t_{k}-1)+\frac{(s-1)(s+2)\eta}{4}+2(2s^{2}-3s+1)\rho_{n}+\gamma=:\phi_{k}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

Using
c_{1}=c_{2}:=\frac{s-2}{8(s-1)}
and
(3s-2)\eta\geq 8(s-1)\rho_{n}
from (
49
), we can derive from (
45
) that

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\beta_{k}&=&\frac{[(c_{1}+c_{2})\eta+2\rho_{n}](t_{k}-s)-\gamma_{k}}{t_{k}}\leq\frac{[(c_{1}+c_{2})\eta+2\rho_{n}](t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}=\big[\frac{(s-2)\eta}{4(s-1)}+2\rho_{n}]\frac{(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}\leq\frac{\eta(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

Since
M^{2}\eta^{2}\leq\frac{t_{k}-s}{t_{k}}
and
s\geq 2
from (
49
), and
\beta_{k}\leq\frac{\eta(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}
, we can upper bound
b_{k}
and
\hat{b}_{k}
from (
48
) respectively as follows:

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}b_{k}&=&\big(\frac{1}{2c_{1}}+M^{2}\eta^{2}\big)\eta t_{k}^{2}\leq\frac{(5s-6)\eta t_{k}^{2}}{s-2},\\
\hat{b}_{k}&=&\big(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}t_{k}}{2c_{2}\eta}+\frac{\gamma(t_{k}-1)}{2t_{k}}+M^{2}\eta^{3}t_{k}\big)t_{k}+\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}\big[\frac{2(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}+(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)\big]\\
&\leq&\frac{4(s-1)\eta(t_{k}-s)^{2}}{(s-2)}+\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)+\frac{\gamma(t_{k}-1)}{2}+\frac{\gamma(s-1)(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}+\frac{(s-1)\gamma(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)}{2}\\
&\leq&\frac{4(s-1)\eta}{(s-2)}[t_{k-1}^{2}-2(s-1)t_{k-1}+(s-1)^{2}]+\eta[t_{k-1}^{2}-(s-2)t_{k-1}-(s-1)]\\
&&+{~}\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}(t_{k-1}^{2}-t_{k-1})+\frac{\gamma t_{k-1}}{2}+(s-1)\gamma\\
&=&\big[\frac{4(s-1)\eta}{(s-2)}+\eta+\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}^{2}-\big[\frac{8(s-1)^{2}\eta}{(s-2)}+(s-2)\eta+\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}-\frac{\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}\\
&&+{~}\eta(s-1)\big[\frac{4(s-1)^{2}}{s-2}-1\big]+(s-1)\gamma\\
&=&\big[\frac{(5s-6)\eta}{s-2}+\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}^{2}-\big[\frac{(9s^{2}-20s+12)\eta}{s-2}+\frac{(s-2)\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}+\frac{(s-1)[(4s^{2}-9s+6)\eta+\gamma(s-2)]}{s-2}\\
&\leq&\big[\frac{(5s-6)\eta}{s-2}+\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}^{2},\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

provided that
\big[\frac{(9s^{2}-20s+12)\eta}{s-2}+\frac{(s-2)\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}\geq\frac{(s-1)[(4s^{2}-9s+6)\eta+\gamma(s-2)]}{s-2}
.

Using these bounds into (
47
), we get

\hskip-2.15277pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathcal{P}_{k+1}&\geq&\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\big[(1-\alpha)\big(|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}-\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big)+\omega L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{(5s-6)\eta t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}-\big[\frac{(5s-6)\eta}{s-2}+\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}^{2}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-8.61108pt

By Young’s inequality, from (
44
) we get
\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\leq 2\|Gx^{k}-Gy^{k-1}\|^{2}+2\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}=2\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}+2\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}
.
Utilizing this relation into the last estimate, we obtain (
50
).
Finally, since
t_{k-1}\geq s
,
s\geq 2
, and
\gamma<16\eta
from (
49
), we can prove that the condition
\big[\frac{(9s^{2}-20s+12)\eta}{s-2}+\frac{(s-2)\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}\geq\frac{(s-1)[(4s^{2}-9s+6)\eta+\gamma(s-2)]}{s-2}
automatically holds.

\square

B.3
The proof of Lemma
6

Since
w^{k}:=Gx^{k}+v^{k}
for
v^{k}\in Tx^{k}
, from (
46
), for any
c_{k}>0
, we can rewrite
\mathcal{P}_{k}
as

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}&=&\frac{a_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-st_{k-1}\langle w^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\frac{s^{2}(s-1)t_{k}}{2\gamma(t_{k}-1)}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+st_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&=&\frac{a_{k}-c_{k}^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2c_{k}^{2}}\|c_{k}^{2}t_{k-1}w^{k}-s(z^{k}-x^{\star})\|^{2}+\frac{s^{2}}{2}\big[\frac{(s-1)t_{k}}{\gamma(t_{k}-1)}-\frac{1}{c_{k}^{2}}\big]\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}st_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle.\end{array}

Let us choose
c_{k}^{2}=\frac{5\gamma}{4(s-1)}
.
Then, we have
a_{k}-c_{k}^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}=\big[\frac{(3s-2)\eta-5\gamma}{4(s-1)}-2\rho_{n}\big]t_{k-1}^{2}+2s\rho_{n}t_{k-1}-\gamma(t_{k-1}-1)
.
Using this relation and noting that
\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\geq-\rho_{*}\|w^{k}\|^{2}
from (
5
) of Assumption
3
, the last expression leads to

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}&\geq&\frac{A_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{10\gamma}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2},\end{array}

which proves (
52
), where
A_{k}:=a_{k}-c_{k}^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}-s\rho_{*}t_{k-1}=\big[\frac{(3s-2)\eta-5\gamma}{4(s-1)}-2\rho_{n}\big]t_{k-1}^{2}+s(2\rho_{n}-\rho_{*})t_{k-1}-\gamma(t_{k-1}-1)
.

\square

Appendix C
Appendix: The Missing Proofs of Lemmas in Section
5

This appendix presents the missing proofs of the technical lemmas in Section
5
.

C.1
The proof of Lemma
7

First, let us choose
\gamma
in (
45
) of Lemma
4
as

\gamma:=\frac{\eta}{s-2}
.

This
leads to the update of
\gamma_{k}
as in (
19
).
In addition,
\varphi
in (
51
) of Lemma
5
becomes

\varphi:=\frac{2(5s-6)\eta}{s-2}+\frac{(s-1)\eta}{s-2}=\frac{(11s-13)\eta}{s-2}
.

Next, under the conditions of Theorem
3.1
and
the choice of
\gamma
as above
, all the conditions of Lemmas
5
and
6
are fulfilled.

Now, since
\rho_{c}=0
and
\alpha=1
, taking the conditional expectation
\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\cdot\big]
on both sides of (
50
) in Lemma
5
, we obtain from this inequality that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k+1}\big]&\geq&\frac{\omega\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\frac{(11s-13)\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(5s-6)}\eta t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\frac{(11s-13)\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Rearranging the last two terms and using
\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]
from the first line of (
15
)
, we have

\hskip-12.91663pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k+1}\big]&\geq&\frac{\omega\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\frac{(11s-13)\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(16s-18)}\eta t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{(11s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}12})\eta t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\frac{(11s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}12})\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-12.91663pt

(88)

Now, multiplying the second line of (
15
) by
\Lambda_{0}\eta t_{k}^{2}
for
\Lambda_{0}
given in (
18
), then rearranging the results, we get

\begin{array}[]{llcl}&0&\geq&\Lambda_{0}\eta t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]-\Lambda_{0}\eta(1-\kappa)t_{k}^{2}\Delta_{k-1}-\Lambda_{0}\Theta L^{2}\eta t_{k}^{2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}-\Lambda_{0}\eta\delta_{k}.\end{array}

Since
t_{k}=k+s+1
, we can show that
t_{k-1}^{2}\leq t_{k}^{2}\leq\frac{(s+1)^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s^{2}}
and
\frac{(s+1)^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s^{2}}-t_{k}^{2}\geq\frac{(2s+1)t_{k-1}(t_{k-1}-s)}{s^{2}}
.
Using these relations, we obtain from the last inequality that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}0&\geq&\frac{\Lambda_{0}(1-\kappa)\eta(s+1)^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]-\frac{\Lambda_{0}\eta(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s^{2}}\Delta_{k-1}-\frac{\Lambda_{0}\Theta L^{2}\eta(s+1)^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s^{2}}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&{~}-\Lambda_{0}\eta\delta_{k}+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Lambda_{0}[s^{2}-(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}]\eta t_{k}^{2}}}{s^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(2s+1)\Lambda_{0}\eta(1-\kappa)t_{k-1}(t_{k-1}-s)}}{s^{2}}\Delta_{k-1}.\end{array}

From (
3
) of Assumption
2
with
\alpha=1
, we also have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}=\|Gx^{k}-Gy^{k-1}\|^{2}\leq L^{2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Combining the last two expressions, we can derive from (
88
) that

\hskip-4.30554pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k+1}\big]&\geq&\frac{\omega\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\Big[\frac{\Lambda_{0}[s^{2}-(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}]}{s^{2}}-\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}16s-18}}{s-2}\Big]\eta t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]+\frac{\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\big(\frac{11s-13}{s-2}+\frac{\Lambda_{0}\Theta(s+1)^{2}}{s^{2}}\big)\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\Lambda_{0}\eta(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]-\frac{\Lambda_{0}\eta(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s^{2}}\Delta_{k-1}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(11s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}12})\eta t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\frac{(11s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}12})\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(2s+1)\Lambda_{0}\eta(1-\kappa)t_{k-1}(t_{k-1}-s)}}{s^{2}}\Delta_{k-1}-\Lambda_{0}\eta\delta_{k}.\end{array}\hskip-12.91663pt

(89)

Next, by our assumption
\frac{2s+1}{(s+1)^{2}}<\kappa\leq 1
in Theorem
3.1
, we have
\Lambda_{0}:=\frac{s^{2}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(16s-18)}}{(s-2)[s^{2}-(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}]}>0
.
In addition, let us denote
\Lambda_{1}:=\frac{2(11s-13)}{s-2}+\frac{2\Lambda_{0}\Theta(s+1)^{2}}{s^{2}}
and choose
\omega:=\Lambda_{1}+1
as in (
19
).
Then, using this
\Lambda_{0}
and
\Lambda_{1}
, the relation
\omega=\Lambda_{1}+1
, and
\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\geq-\rho_{*}\|w^{k}\|^{2}
from Assumption
3
, we can show from (
89
) that

{\hskip-4.30554pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k+1}\big]&\geq&\frac{\omega\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\big[\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}-s(s-1)\rho_{*}\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{\omega\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\Lambda_{0}(1-\kappa)\eta(s+1)^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]-\frac{\Lambda_{0}\eta(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s^{2}}\Delta_{k-1}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(11s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}12})\eta t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\frac{(11s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}12})\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(2s+1)\Lambda_{0}\eta(1-\kappa)t_{k-1}(t_{k-1}-s)}}{s^{2}}\Delta_{k-1}+\frac{\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}-\Lambda_{0}\eta\delta_{k}.\end{array}\hskip-12.91663pt}\vskip-2.15277pt

(90)

Now, note that since
0\leq\rho_{*}\leq\rho_{n}
, for
k\geq 0
, we have

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\phi_{k}-2\rho_{*}s(s-1)&=&\big[\frac{s^{2}-4s-2}{2}\eta-4(s-1)\rho_{n}\big](k+s)+\frac{(s-1)(s-2)\eta}{4}\\
&&+{~}2(2s-1)(s-1)\rho_{n}+\frac{\eta}{s-2}-2\rho_{*}s(s-1)\\
&\geq&\big[\frac{s^{2}-4s-2}{2}\eta-4(s-1)\rho_{n}\big](k+1)+\big[\frac{s^{2}-4s-2}{2}\eta-4(s-1)\rho_{n}\big](s-1)\\
&&+{~}\frac{(s-1)(s-2)\eta}{4}+2(2s-1)(s-1)\rho_{n}+\frac{\eta}{s-2}-2\rho_{n}s(s-1)\\
&=&\big[\frac{s^{2}-4s-2}{2}\eta-4(s-1)\rho_{n}\big](k+1)-(s-1)\big[2(s-1)\rho_{n}-\frac{(2s^{2}-7s-6)\eta}{4}\big]+\frac{\eta}{s-2}\\
&\geq&\big[\frac{s^{2}-4s-2}{4}\eta-2(s-1)\rho_{n}\big](k+1)+\big[\frac{s^{2}-4s-2+(s-1)(2s^{2}-7s-6)}{4}+\frac{1}{s-2}\big]\eta\\
&&-{~}2s(s-1)\rho_{n}\\
&\geq&\frac{[(s^{2}-4s-2)\eta-8(s-1)\rho_{n}](k+1)}{4}>0.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

Here, we have used the condition
8(s-1)\rho_{n}\leq(3s-2)\eta
from (
49
) and
s\geq 7
to prove that

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}(s^{2}-4s-2)\eta-8(s-1)\rho_{n}&\geq&s(s-7)\eta>0,\\
\big[\frac{s^{2}-4s-2+(s-1)(2s^{2}-7s-6)}{4}+\frac{1}{s-2}\big]\eta&\geq&2s(s-1)\rho_{n}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

Furthermore, since
t_{k}\geq s+1
, to guarantee
\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\leq 1
, we impose
(s+1)M^{2}\eta^{2}\leq 1
.
However, since
\omega
is given in (
19
), the condition
(s+1)M^{2}\eta^{2}\leq 1
is guaranteed if we choose
\eta\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{2(1+\omega)(s+1)}}
.
In addition, since
\eta\geq\frac{8(s-1)\rho_{n}}{3s-2}
from (
49
), we finally get

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\frac{8(s-1)\rho_{n}}{3s-2}\leq\eta\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{2(1+\omega)(s+1)}}=\frac{\lambda}{L},\quad\textrm{where}\quad\lambda:=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2(1+\omega)(s+1)}}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

This inequality holds if we choose
\eta
as in (
19
).

Finally, if we recall
\mathcal{L}_{k}
from (
53
), then using the last relations and
\epsilon_{1}:=1-(s+1)M^{2}\eta^{2}\geq 0
, (
90
) leads to (
54
).

\square

C.2
The proof of Lemma
8

First, from the first and second lines of (
VFOG
), we can easily derive that

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}v^{k}&:=&s(z^{k}-x^{k})=t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})+t_{k}(\eta-\beta_{k})(\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k-1}).\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

(91)

Using this expression, the third line of (
VFOG
), and
x^{k+1}-y^{k}=-\eta(\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k}-e^{k-1})
, we can derive

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}v^{k+1}-\frac{(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}v^{k}&=&v^{k+1}-v^{k}+\frac{s}{t_{k}}v^{k}\\
&=&s(z^{k+1}-z^{k})-s(x^{k+1}-x^{k})+s(y^{k}-x^{k})+s(\eta-\beta_{k})(\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k-1})\\
&=&s(z^{k+1}-z^{k})-s(x^{k+1}-y^{k})+s(\eta-\beta_{k})(\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k-1})\\
&=&-\gamma_{k}(\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k-1})+s\eta(\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k}-e^{k-1})+s(\eta-\beta_{k})(\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k-1})\\
&=&s\eta(\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k})+[s(\eta-\beta_{k})-\gamma_{k}]\hat{w}^{k}+s\eta e^{k}-(s\beta_{k}+\gamma_{k})e^{k-1}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

Denote
d^{k}:=\frac{t_{k}}{s}\big[s\eta(\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k})+[s(\eta-\beta_{k})-\gamma_{k}]\hat{w}^{k}+s\eta e^{k}-(s\beta_{k}+\gamma_{k})e^{k-1}\big]
.
Then, the last estimate is rewritten as
v^{k+1}=\big(1-\frac{s}{t_{k}}\big)v^{k}+\frac{s}{t_{k}}d^{k}
.
By convexity of
\|\cdot\|^{2}
,
\frac{s}{t_{k}}\in(0,1]
, and Young’s inequality, we have

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|v^{k+1}\|^{2}&\leq&\frac{t_{k}-s}{t_{k}}\|v^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{s}{t_{k}}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&\|v^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{s}{t_{k}}\|v^{k}\|^{2}+4s\eta^{2}t_{k}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{4[s(\eta-\beta_{k})-\gamma_{k}]^{2}t_{k}}{s}\|\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}4s\eta^{2}t_{k}\|e^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{4(s\beta_{k}+\gamma_{k})^{2}t_{k}}{s}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

Note that from Lemma
5
, we have
\beta_{k}\leq\frac{\eta(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}\leq\eta
and
\gamma_{k}=\frac{\eta(t_{k}-1)}{(s-2)t_{k}}\leq\eta
.
Using these inequalities into the last estimate and taking the full expectation on both sides of the resulting inequality, we get

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathbb{E}\big[\|v^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]&\leq&\|v^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{s}{t_{k}}\mathbb{E}\big[\|v^{k}\|^{2}\big]+4s\eta^{2}t_{k}\mathbb{E}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{4(2s+1)^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{s}\mathbb{E}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\big]+4s\eta^{2}t_{k}\mathbb{E}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{4(s+1)^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{s}\mathbb{E}\big[\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}\big].\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

(92)

Applying Lemma
11
to the last inequality by using the summability bounds in (
55
), we get

{\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[\|v^{k}\|^{2}\big]=\lim_{k\to\infty}s^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k}-z^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0,\ \ \textrm{and}\ \ \sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\tfrac{s}{t_{k}}\mathbb{E}\big[\|z^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}\big]<+\infty.}\vskip-2.15277pt

These relations imply (
56
).

Next, since
t_{k}(x^{k+1}-y^{k})=-\eta t_{k}\big(\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k}-e^{k-1}\big)
by (
VFOG
), by Young’s inequality, we can show that

{\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&3\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}+3\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}+3\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt

Combining this inequality and (
55
), we can prove that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]&<&+\infty.\end{array}

This is exactly the first summability bound of (
57
).

Finally, using again (
91
) and then applying Young’s inequality, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&\frac{3s^{2}}{t_{k}}\|z^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}+6\eta^{2}t_{k}\|\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}+6\eta^{2}t_{k}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2},\\
t_{k}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&2t_{k}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}+2t_{k}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Utilizing the first line of (
57
), (
55
), and the second summability bound of (
56
), we can also derive from these inequalities that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}\mathbb{E}\big[\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}\big]<+\infty\quad\text{and}\quad\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\big]<+\infty.\end{array}

These prove the last two lines of (
57
), respectively.

\square

C.3
The proof of Lemma
9

Since
\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k}\big]<+\infty
, from (
52
) of Lemma
6
, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\frac{A_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{10\gamma}\mathbb{E}\big[\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\big]\leq\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k}\big]<+\infty.\end{array}

This inequality implies that
\mathbb{E}\big[\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\big]\leq M:=\frac{10\gamma\mathcal{L}_{0}}{s^{2}(s-1)}
.

Now, from Young’s inequality, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}-\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{s}\langle\hat{e}^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle&\leq&\frac{\gamma_{k}t_{k}^{2}}{s}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\gamma_{k}}{st_{k}^{2}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2},\\
-\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{s}\langle w^{k},z^{k}-x^{k}\rangle&\leq&\frac{\gamma_{k}t_{k}}{s}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\gamma_{k}}{st_{k}}\|z^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}\leq\frac{\eta t_{k}}{s}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{st_{k}}\|z^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Using Young’s inequality in
①
, the condition (
5
) from Assumption
3
in
②
, and the last two relations, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|z^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}&=&\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+2\langle z^{k+1}-z^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{s}\langle w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\frac{\gamma_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\|w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tiny\textcircled{1}}}{{\leq}}&\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{s}\langle w^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{s}\langle\hat{e}^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\frac{2\gamma_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{2\gamma_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{s}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{s}\langle w^{k},z^{k}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\frac{\gamma_{k}t_{k}^{2}}{s}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\gamma_{k}}{st_{k}^{2}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\frac{2\gamma_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{2\gamma_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tiny\textcircled{2}}}{{\leq}}&\big(1+\frac{\eta}{st_{k}^{2}}\big)\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\big(\frac{\eta t_{k}}{s}+\frac{\eta\rho_{*}}{s}+\frac{2\eta^{2}}{s^{2}}\big)\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta}{st_{k}}\|z^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}+\big(\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{s}+\frac{2\eta^{2}}{s^{2}}\big)\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

(93)

Taking the full expectation on both sides of this inequality and noting that the last three terms are summable, applying Lemma
11
, we conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\big]
exists.
However, since
\lim_{k}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k}-z^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0
by (
56
), using the triangle inequality
|\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|-\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\||\leq\|x^{k}-z^{k}\|
, we conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\big]
also exists.

Since
\{t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\}
is bounded, there exists
\bar{M}>0
such that
t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\bar{M}^{2}
.
Using this inequality and
\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k}-z^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0
from (
56
), we can show that when
k\to\infty
:

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k-1}^{2}|\mathbb{E}\big[\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle\big]|^{2}&\leq&t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k}-z^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\bar{M}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k}-z^{k}\|^{2}\big]\to 0.\end{array}

This expression implies that
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}\mathbb{E}\big[\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle\big]=0
.
From (
46
), we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}=\frac{a_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+st_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle+\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Since
\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k}\big]
and
\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\big]
exist and
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}\mathbb{E}\big[\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle\big]=0
, we conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}a_{k}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]
exists.
Since
a_{k}=\mathcal{O}\left(t_{k-1}^{2}\right)
, we conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]
exists.
However, since
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k-1}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]<+\infty
by our assumption, we can easily argue that
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0
, which proves the first limit of (
58
).

Note that
\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}=\|Gx^{k}-Gy^{k-1}\|^{2}\leq L^{2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}
by Assumption
2
.
Utilizing this relation and the first line of (
57
), we can conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0
.

Finally, by Young’s inequality, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k-1}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&2t_{k-1}^{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+2t_{k-1}^{2}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Since
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0
and
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0
, the last inequality implies that
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0
, which proves the second limit of (
58
).

\square

C.4
The proof of Lemma
10

First, if we choose
\gamma:=\frac{\eta}{s-2}
, then
\varphi
in (
50
) becomes
\varphi=\frac{2(5s-6)\eta}{s-2}+(s-1)\gamma=\frac{(11s-13)\eta}{s-2}
.
Second, applying (
3
) from Assumption
2
with
x=x^{k+1}
and
y=y^{k}
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\tau\omega L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}&\geq&\tau\omega(1-\alpha)|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}+\tau\omega\alpha\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2},\quad\tau\in(0,1].\end{array}

Using
|\!\|\cdot|\!\|\geq\|\cdot\|
and this inequality, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{[1]}&:=&(1-\alpha)\big(|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}-\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big)+\omega L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&\tau\omega(1-\alpha)|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}+\tau\omega\alpha\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+(1-\tau)\omega L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Next, substituting this relation into (
50
) of Lemma
5
, we can derive that

\hskip-2.15277pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathcal{P}_{k+1}&\geq&\frac{\tau\omega\alpha\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{\tau\omega(1-\alpha)\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(1-\tau)\omega\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}+\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{(11s-13)\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{(5s-6)\eta t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{(11s-13)\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-8.61108pt

(94)

Since
w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}=Gx^{k+1}-Gy^{k}
and
w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}=Gx^{k}-Gy^{k-1}
, by Young’s inequality, the relation
|\!\|\cdot|\!\|\geq\|\cdot\|
, and (
3
) from Assumption
2
, we can derive that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\geq\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2},\\
|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}+|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}+|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\geq\frac{1}{3}|\!\|Gy^{k}-Gy^{k-1}|\!\|^{2},\\
L^{2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}\geq\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|.\end{array}

(95)

Then, since
t_{k}^{2}\geq t_{k}(t_{k}-s)
and
t_{k-1}^{2}\geq t_{k}(t_{k}-s)
for
s\geq 2
, using the last two inequalities and the conditions

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\tau\omega(1-\alpha)\eta\geq 4\rho_{c}\quad\textrm{and}\quad(1-\tau)\omega\eta\geq\frac{2(11s-12)\eta}{s-2},\end{array}

(96)

we can prove that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{[1]}&:=&\frac{\tau\omega(1-\alpha)\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}+\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(1-\tau)\omega\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{(2s-13)\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\overset{\tiny\eqref{eq:para_cond10_proof}}{\geq}&\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}+\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}+\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}-\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k-1}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(11s-12)\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{(2s-12)\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\overset{\tiny\eqref{eq:three_estimates_proof1}}{\geq}&\frac{\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{3}|\!\|Gy^{k}-Gy^{k-1}|\!\|^{2}+\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}-\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k-1}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(11s-12)\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{(2s-12)\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Substituting this expression into (
94
) and taking the conditional expectation
\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\cdot\big]
on both sides of the result, and splitting
\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]

and
\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}
into two parts, we can show that

\hskip-8.61108pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k+1}\big]&\geq&\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\tau\omega\alpha\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{(11s-12)\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\frac{(11s-12)\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}-\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k-1}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{3}|\!\|Gy^{k}-Gy^{k-1}|\!\|^{2}+\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\frac{(11s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}13})\eta t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\frac{(11s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}13})\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big].\end{array}\hskip-30.1388pt

(97)

Now, for a given
\bar{c}\in(0,1)
, we can derive from (
26
) of Definition
2
that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]&\leq&\frac{(1-\kappa_{k})t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}t_{k-1}^{2}}t_{k-1}^{2}\Delta_{k-1}-\frac{1-\bar{c}}{\bar{c}}t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]+\frac{\Theta_{k}t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}}|\!\|Gy^{k}-Gy^{k-1}|\!\|^{2}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~+~\frac{\delta_{k}}{\bar{c}}}.\end{array}

(98)

Suppose that there exists
\epsilon_{2}\geq 0
such that
\frac{(1-\kappa_{k})t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}t_{k-1}^{2}}\leq\frac{1-\bar{c}}{\bar{c}}-\epsilon_{2}
, which holds if
\bar{c}\leq\frac{1}{1+\epsilon_{2}}\big[1-\frac{(1-\kappa_{k})t_{k}^{2}}{t_{k-1}^{2}}\big]
.
Moreover,
to handle the term
|\!\|Gy^{k}-Gy^{k-1}|\!\|^{2}
,
we also need to impose
\frac{\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{3}-\frac{[(5s-6)\eta+(s-2)\varphi]\Theta_{k}t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}(s-2)}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=\frac{\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{3}-\frac{(16s-19)\eta\Theta_{k}t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}(s-2)}}\geq 0
, leading to
\bar{c}\geq\frac{3(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta\Theta_{k}t_{k}}{\rho_{c}(s-2)(t_{k}-s)}
.
Combining both conditions on
\bar{c}
, we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\frac{3(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta\Theta_{k}t_{k}}{\rho_{c}(s-2)(t_{k}-s)}\leq\bar{c}\leq\frac{1}{1+\epsilon_{2}}\big[1-\frac{(1-\kappa_{k})t_{k}^{2}}{t_{k-1}^{2}}\big].\end{array}

This is exactly the condition (
74
).
In this case, using
\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]
from (
26
) of Definition
2
, (
98
) becomes

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\frac{(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]&\leq&\frac{(1-\bar{c})(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{(s-2)\bar{c}}\Delta_{k-1}-\frac{(1-\bar{c})(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta t_{k}^{2}}{(s-2)\bar{c}}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~+~\frac{(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta\delta_{k}}{\bar{c}(s-2)}}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta\Theta t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}(s-2)}|\!\|Gy^{k}-Gy^{k-1}|\!\|^{2}-\frac{(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta\epsilon_{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\Delta_{k-1}.\end{array}

Substituting this expression into (
97
)
and then using the
L
-Lipschitz continuity of
G
and the condition
\frac{\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{3}-\frac{(16s-19)\eta\Theta_{k}t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}(s-2)}\geq 0
, we obtain

\hskip-2.15277pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k+1}\big]&\geq&\frac{(11s-12)\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\frac{(11s-12)\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}-\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k-1}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(11s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}13})\eta t_{k}^{2}}{s-2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\frac{(11s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}13})\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(1-\bar{c})(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta t_{k}^{2}}{(s-2)\bar{c}}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]-\frac{(1-\bar{c})(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{(s-2)\bar{c}}\Delta_{k-1}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\tau\omega\alpha\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}+\frac{(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta\epsilon_{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\Delta_{k-1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~-~\frac{(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta\delta_{k}}{\bar{c}(s-2)}}.\end{array}\hskip-4.30554pt

Recall the Lyapunov function
\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}
from (
75
) as follows:

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}&:=&\frac{a_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+st_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle+\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\rho_{c}t_{k-1}^{2}|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(11s-12)\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}+\frac{(11s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}13})t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}+\frac{(1-\bar{c})(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{(s-2)\bar{c}}\Delta_{k-1}.\end{array}

Using this function, the last expression becomes

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}-\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k+1}\big]&\geq&\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\tau\omega\alpha\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}+\frac{(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta\epsilon_{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s-2}\Delta_{k-1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~-~\frac{(16s-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}19})\eta\delta_{k}}{\bar{c}(s-2)}}.\end{array}

This exactly proves (
76
)
, with
\tau
chosen as follows
.

If we choose
\tau:=\frac{4\rho_{c}}{(1-\alpha)\omega\eta}\in(0,1)
, then both conditions in (
96
) hold, provided that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\omega\geq\frac{4\rho_{c}}{(1-\alpha)\eta}+\frac{2(11s-12)}{s-2}.\end{array}

(99)

Since
\rho_{c}\leq\rho_{n}
from Assumption
4
and
(3s-2)\eta\geq 8(s-1)\rho_{n}
from (
49
), the condition (
99
) holds if we choose
\omega=\hat{\omega}:=\frac{3s-2}{2(1-\alpha)(s-1)}+\frac{2(11s-12)}{s-2}
as in (
29
).

To guarantee the nonnegativity of the
coefficients on the
right-hand side of (
76
), we need to impose
\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\leq 1
.
Since
t_{k}\geq s+1
, the last condition holds if
(s+1)M^{2}\eta^{2}\leq 1
.
However, since
\hat{\omega}
is given in (
29
), the condition
(s+1)M^{2}\eta^{2}\leq 1
is guaranteed if we choose
\eta\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{2(s+1)(1+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hat{\omega}})}}
.
Since
\eta\geq\frac{8(s-1)\rho_{n}}{3s-2}
, we finally get
\frac{8(s-1)\rho_{n}}{3s-2}\leq\eta\leq\frac{\hat{\lambda}}{L}
as in (
73
) with
\hat{\lambda}:=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2(s+1)(1+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hat{\omega}})}}
.

Moreover, utilizing (
45
) and Lemma
5
with
c_{1}=c_{2}=\frac{s-2}{8(s-1)}
,
t_{k}=k+s+1
, and
\gamma=\frac{\eta}{s-2}
as before, we also have
\gamma_{k}:=\frac{\gamma(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}=\frac{\eta(k+s)}{(s-2)(k+s+1)}
and
\beta_{k}=\big[\frac{(s-2)\eta}{4(s-1)}+2\rho_{n}]\frac{(k+1)}{k+s+1}-\frac{\gamma_{k}}{k+s+1}
as in (
73
).
Since
\gamma=\frac{\eta}{s-2}
, we also have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\phi_{k}&=&\big[\frac{(s-2)}{2}\eta-4(s-1)\rho_{n}-3\gamma\big](k+s)+\frac{(s-1)(s-2)\eta}{4}+2(2s^{2}-3s+1)\rho_{n}+\gamma\\
&=&\big[\frac{s^{2}-4s-2}{2(s-2)}\eta-4(s-1)\rho_{n}\big](k+s)+(s-1)\big[\frac{(s-2)\eta}{4}+2(2s-1)\rho_{n}\big]+\frac{\eta}{s-2}\end{array}

as desired.

\square

C.5
The proof of Corollary
1
: Oracle complexity of
VFOG
using (
16
)

Assume that
\widetilde{G}y^{k}
satisfies the condition (
15
) with
\kappa=1
,
\Theta=0
, and
\delta_{k}:=\frac{\delta}{t_{k}^{1+\nu}}
for a given
\delta>0
and
\nu>0
.
Then, we have
\mathcal{S}_{\infty}:=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\delta_{k}=\delta\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{(k+s+1)^{1+\nu}}<+\infty
.
Moreover, by (
15
) we also have
\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\frac{\delta_{k}}{t_{k}^{2}}=\frac{\delta}{t_{k}^{3+\nu}}
.
However, since
\widetilde{G}y^{k}
is constructed by (
16
), we have
\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\frac{\sigma^{2}}{b_{k}}
, where
\sigma^{2}
is the variance bound in Assumption
1
.
Therefore, the condition
\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\frac{\delta}{t_{k}^{3+\nu}}
holds if we impose
\frac{\sigma^{2}}{b_{k}}\leq\frac{\delta}{t_{k}^{3+\nu}}
.
Hence, we can choose
b_{k}:=\big\lfloor\frac{\sigma^{2}t_{k}^{3+\nu}}{\delta}\big\rfloor=\big\lfloor\frac{\sigma^{2}(k+s)^{3+\nu}}{\delta}\big\rfloor
as stated.
In this case, the expected total number of oracle calls
\mathbf{G}_{\xi}
is at most

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{T}_{K}\big]&:=&\sum_{k=0}^{K}b_{k}\leq\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\delta}\sum_{k=0}^{K}(k+s)^{3+\nu}=\mathcal{O}\big(\frac{\sigma^{2}K^{4+\nu}}{\delta}\big).\end{array}

By (
22
) of Theorem
3.1
, the number of iterations
K
to reach an
\epsilon
-solution in expectation is
K=\mathcal{O}\left(\epsilon^{-1}\right)
.
Therefore, we obtain
\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{T}_{K}\big]=\mathcal{O}\big(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\delta\epsilon^{4+\nu}}\big)
as stated.
The expected total number of
J_{\eta T}
evaluations is the same as
K
, which is
\mathcal{O}\left(\epsilon^{-1}\right)
.

Alternatively, if we choose
b_{k}:=\big\lfloor\frac{\sigma^{2}(k+s)^{3}\log(k+s)}{\delta}\big\rfloor
, then
\mathcal{S}_{K}:=\delta\sum_{k=0}^{K}\frac{1}{(k+s)\log(k+s)}\leq 1+\log(\log(K+s))
.
Hence, from (
22
) of Theorem
3.1
, to achieve
\mathbb{E}\big[\|Gx^{k}+v^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\epsilon^{2}
, we requires
\frac{C_{0}(\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}+\Lambda_{0}S_{K})}{(K+s)^{2}}\leq\epsilon^{2}
.
Using the upper bound of
\mathcal{S}_{K}
, we can show that
K=\mathcal{O}\big(\frac{1}{\epsilon\sqrt{\log(\epsilon^{-1})}}\big)
.
Using this estimation of
K
, we can estimate that
\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{T}_{K}\big]=\sum_{k=0}^{K}b_{k}\leq\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\delta}\sum_{k=0}^{K}(k+s)^{3}\log(k+s)\leq\frac{\sigma^{2}(K+s)^{4}\log(K+s)}{4\delta}=\mathcal{O}\big(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{4}\log(\epsilon^{-1})}\big)
.
The expected total number of
J_{\eta T}
evaluations is the same as
K
, which is
\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\epsilon^{-1})
.

\square
